# Gap map — KDE Plasma 6 / Breeze (`kde-breeze`)

Model at caeddd6b (`docs/property-registry.toml`, `native-theme/src/kde/`). Method: `docs/todo_v0.7.1_model-completeness-gap-map.md` §2–§4. This file collects no values: where a cell quotes a constant or a factor, it was read at the cited line, and it is there only to say *what* the gap is.

## Sources and abbreviations

All sources are the KDE GitHub mirrors, shallow clones of `master` read on 2026-10-27. **Before values are collected, every citation must be re-pinned to the release tags the rest of `platform-facts.md` uses (Breeze/KWin v6.7.x, KF 6.31.0), because line numbers move.**

Source kinds: **read** (a key the reader can query), **pub** (published source), **measure** (on the maintainer's Plasma 6 desktop: a Qt Widgets probe app and a Kirigami/QQC2 probe app, captured with Spectacle / KWin's ScreenShot2 D-Bus interface; animations by screen recording at the display refresh rate, or by Qt's animation trace). "Model today" names the nearest field; *partial* means a field exists but cannot hold what Breeze draws.

Recurring Breeze facts the rows lean on (so they need not be repeated): `focusColor` = `[Colors:View] DecorationFocus`, `hoverColor` = `[Colors:View] DecorationHover` (`hp.h:64-73`); `separatorColor` = mix(Window, WindowText, frameContrast) (`hp:329-332`); `frameBackgroundColor` = mix(Window, Base, `Blend_Value`) (`hp:200-203`); `arrowColor` = mix(text role, its background, `arrowShade` 0.15) (`hp:38`, `:206-219`); the neutral-highlight variant is any widget carrying the `_kde_highlight_neutral` property (`st:8751-8767`; `breezepropertynames.cpp:18`); `hasHighlightNeutral` recolours frames to `[Colors:View] ForegroundNeutral`.

---

Checked, no gap: a QTextEdit's frame hover/focus outline is Breeze's frame-shadow overlay (`kstyle/breezeframeshadow.cpp`; `st:3959-3965`) in the line edit's colours, so `text_area` adds nothing beyond K1.40–K1.45.

## 4.3 Properties that cross widgets

### Shadows and elevation

## Data and source discrepancies noticed (not model gaps)

These are fields the model has whose KDE source in `platform-facts.md` or the reader disagrees with the Breeze source read above; each needs checking before values are collected.

| ID | Field | Discrepancy | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| D1 | `menu.hover_background`, `menu.hover_text_color` | Facts: `[Colors:Selection]` background/foreground. Breeze: `focusColor` (View `DecorationFocus`) at α `Blend_Value` + outline, text stays `WindowText` (the solid colour is the *pressed* item) | `st:6019-6042`, `st:6154-6155` |
| D2 | `window.title_bar_background`, `inactive_*` | Reader reads `[WM]`; KWin uses `[Colors:Header]` / `[Colors:Header][Inactive]` when the scheme has them (K1.13) | `kwin src/decorations/decorationpalette.cpp:126-160` |
| D3 | `defaults.selection_inactive_background` | Facts: "(none) — unchanged on focus loss". KColorScheme (Breeze default `ChangeSelectionColor=true`) tints the Window background with the Selection colour for the Inactive group | `kcs:303-321`, `kcs:386-389`; `colors` BreezeLight `:18-19` |
| D4 | `list.header_background` | Reader: `[Colors:Header]`. Breeze's header section fills `QPalette::Button` | `st:6827-6840` |
| D5 | `combo_box.border.shadow_enabled` | Facts: "no shadow". A non-editable combo is a button frame with the button's micro-shadow | `st:7851-7866`; `hp:729-732` |
| D6 | `list.hover_background` | Facts: "DecorationHover blend". Breeze: `Highlight` at α `Blend_Value` | `st:4870-4875` |
| D7 | `defaults.disabled_text_color` | Reader: `[Colors:View] ForegroundInactive`; facts: Disabled Window `ForegroundNormal` via `[ColorEffects:Disabled]` | `native-theme/src/kde/colors.rs:493`; `kcs:21-56` |
| D8 | `input.caret_color` | Facts: `DecorationFocus`. Qt draws the line-edit caret in the palette's text colour: `QLineEdit::paintEvent` sets the pen to `Text` and the control draws the caret with it | pub qtbase v6.12.0 `qlineedit.cpp:1985`, `:2003-2006`; `qwidgetlinecontrol.cpp:633-638`; measure: probe app (review 2026-10-28: settled) |
| D9 | `defaults.focus_ring_*` | Facts: 1.001 px stroke, 2 px margin. Breeze's focus frame is a 2 px *filled* ring at `Highlight` α 0.3 (K3.20) | `st:6774-6781` |

## Cross-check (pass 2, 2026-10-28)

Pass 2 checked this map against the other five surveys (`docs/todo_v0.7.1_model-completeness-gap-map.md` §6.1–§6.3): the controls they list that this file did not (A), every capability C01–C28 (B), and every "(none)" cell of the KDE column of `platform-facts.md` (C). New rows carry a "†" after their ID: 15 in §4.1, 16 in §4.2, 11 in §4.3.

Sources read in this pass beyond the table at the top. The KDE clones are the same commits; KWin's sparse clone gained `src/outline.cpp`, `src/outputconfigurationstore.cpp`, `src/inputmethod.cpp`, `src/qml/outline/` and the plugins `dialogparent`, `frozenapp`, `fullscreen`, `systembell`, `shakecursor`, `startupfeedback` (and, read for enablement only, `sheet`, `invert`, `colorblindnesscorrection`, `translucency`, `slidingnotifications`, `blendchanges`, `screenedge`, `highlightwindow`, `zoom`, `slideback`), all at KDE/kwin @ `f130ab33` (tag `v6.8.2`). Fetched files, read 2026-10-28: qtbase at tag `v6.12.0` (`src/widgets/…`, `src/gui/kernel/qplatformtheme.cpp`, `qplatformintegration.cpp`), cited as "qtbase v6.12.0 `<file>:<line>`"; KDE/kio @ `2dd4bdc916a6f46d297d8788f5b628181483315d` (tag `v6.31.0`), KDE/kcompletion @ `b9094f8773b8e143ffd3793a933157b72930499d` (tag `v6.31.0`), KDE/ktexteditor @ `ea14509c132f2c016d55b38f7a1ec24cc6f9e7d2` (tag `v6.31.0`), KDE/sonnet @ `faff0a83f637b74e900d82252aab8fccf99ae0d7` (tag `v6.31.0`), KDE/xdg-desktop-portal-kde @ `5b33864bf4ba72acca9b5965469c617a13512865` (tag `v6.8.2`) (read 2026-10-28 at the `master` heads of that day, re-pinned 2026-10-30, §6.4).

### A. Controls other surveys list

Decision: 1 = KDE draws it as a standalone control (row in §4.2); 2 = KDE draws it inside an element this map already covers (rows named; rows added where they missed something); 3 = KDE has no such control.

| Control | Decision | Rows added, or reason and source |
|---|---|---|
| search field | 2 | Quick: Q1.15–Q1.17 (qqc2 and Kirigami SearchField, its popup); Qt Widgets has no search-field class (a QLineEdit with a clear button: K1.43, K1.46†) — `kwa src/` widget list, qtbase |
| date picker | 1 | K2.4 (the calendar) and K2.23† (KDateComboBox, KDatePickerPopup, QDateEdit's calendar popup, KDateTable cell colours) |
| pull-to-refresh | 1 | Q2.54† (Kirigami ScrollablePage's PullDownIndicator); its arc is Q1.121 |
| split button | 2 | K1.155 (QToolButton `MenuButtonPopup`: split sub-control with a separator line); Qt and KF have no split push button |
| carousel | 3 | No carousel in KF6: none in the `kwa src/` widget list, Kirigami's `src/controls/`, or qqc2-desktop-style's control list (`qqc2-desktop-style@2efe38d5 CMakeLists.txt:110-168`); the nearest part, PageIndicator, is Q2.11 |
| colour picker | 1 | K2.25† (QColorDialog shown through the KDE platform theme; KColorCombo); K2.20 is the colour button |
| find bar | 1 | K2.30† (KTextEditor's search bar: match / no-match field backgrounds, search and replace highlights) |
| menu button | 2 | K1.27 (QPushButton with a menu), Q1.8 (qqc2 `menu` arrow flag), K1.155 (tool-button menu arrows) |
| navigation drawer/pane | 2 | Q1.53–Q1.66 (Kirigami OverlayDrawer, GlobalDrawer incl. collapsed rail, ContextDrawer), Q1.67 (qqc2 Drawer) |

| time picker | 1 | K2.24† (KTimeComboBox, QTimeEdit, KDateTimeEdit) |
| token field | 3 | No token field in KF6 (`kwa src/` widget list, Kirigami `src/controls/`); tags are drawn as Kirigami Chips (Q2.22–Q2.24) |
| view switcher | 2 | Q2.33–Q2.36 (Kirigami NavigationTabBar), K2.14 (KPageView's Tabbed and List faces) |
| access key badge | 3 | KDE marks access keys by underlined mnemonics, not badges: K3.44 (Breeze `MnemonicsMode`), Q1.10 (Kirigami mnemonics, shown while Alt is held) |

| writing tools | 3 | Apple Intelligence; no counterpart in KF6 |
| zoom button | 3 | WinUI SemanticZoom; no semantic-zoom control in Qt Widgets, KF6 or Kirigami (same lists) |

Tally: 1 — 12 controls (rows K2.23†–K2.32†, Q2.53†, Q2.54†, Q2.58†); 2 — 20; 3 — 18 (review 2026-10-28: was 2 — 19, 3 — 19, before "scrollbar annotation" moved to 2). Q2.55†–Q2.57† (Kirigami Forms, About page) were found on the way, under controls KDE already had rows for (preference rows / boxed list, about dialog).

### Found wrong in pass 1 (not rewritten here)

- `platform-facts.md@fc6109d1:1265` (menu items rectangular) and `:1622` (combo, no shadow) disagree with Breeze's source (K1.60, K1.244 / D5) — for `docs/todo.md`'s list of values against their sources.
- The Link note above cites "Q1.154–Q1.156" for the Quick link rows; they are Q1.134–Q1.136.
- In `tags-kde.tsv`, several §4.2 rows sit under a control name that is not theirs, which hid them from the cross-check: K2.7 (command link) under "button group/toggle group", K2.15 (KTitleWidget) under "status page/placeholder", K2.16 (KMultiTabBar) under "navigation rail", K2.17 (splitter collapser) under "button group/toggle group", K2.22 (wizard) under "about dialog", Q2.43 (drag handle) under "icon button"; K2.2 (tool button) has no tag line at all, so "tool button" came out as missing for KDE.
- The cross-check list's "tool button" entry for GNOME is GN-2.42, which is GTK's text selection handles and magnifier.
- The Kirigami Forms module (`src/forms/`, cards and flat styles: KDE's boxed list) and the KIO file widgets were not surveyed in pass 1 (Q2.55†–Q2.57†, K1.142†, K2.26†–K2.27†). kirigami-addons (FormCard, date and time pickers), which many KDE applications use, is outside this map's scope as written; whether it belongs is a question for the rationale.

Rows for the four seed groups no row covered after pass 2 (G12, G19, G24, G27; the main

are the row's capability tags (main map §4.3) and source kinds.

### Code editor and rich text (G12, G27)

**Code editor.** KTextEditor (KF 6.31.0, the editor part of Kate, KWrite and KDevelop) draws with a
KSyntaxHighlighting theme. Breeze ships two, *Breeze Light* and *Breeze Dark*
(`ksh/data/themes/breeze-light.theme`, `breeze-dark.theme`). The search bar and its highlights are
K2.30†; the misspelling underline K1.49†.

| K-SF01† | 4.2 | code editor | Which theme draws | "Auto Color Theme Selection" is on by default; the editor then takes the KSyntaxHighlighting theme whose `BackgroundColor` equals the palette's `Base` (and, among those, whose `TextSelection` equals `Highlight`), else Breeze Dark when `Base`'s lightness < 128, else Breeze Light. With Breeze's colour schemes this lands on the Breeze themes; with any other scheme on Breeze Light/Dark unless a theme matches its colours | P: `kte/src/utils/kateconfig.cpp:691`, `:893-904`; `ksh/src/lib/repository.cpp:176-210`; R: `[Colors:View] BackgroundNormal`, `[Colors:Selection] BackgroundNormal` (kdeglobals) decide the match | none | C05, C25 | R, P |

**Rich text / Markdown.** Kirigami has no Markdown component (`grep -ri markdown` over
`kirigami@eecf183a src/` finds none); KDE applications render Markdown through Qt's
`QTextDocument` (Qt Widgets `Qt::MarkdownText`, Qt Quick `Text.MarkdownText`), whose look is Qt's,

### Drop zones and dialog placement (G19, G24)

#### G19 — drop zone under a dragged pane, tab or dock

Covered by: K2.12 (Breeze's `QRubberBand` paint: fill, outline, radius, the mask rule, its main-window and
graphics-view cases included since its correction of 2026-10-28; review 2026-10-28: "see the row below for the case
K2.12's wording leaves out" no longer held), K3.38† (the dock re-layout slide of a QMainWindow, timed by
`SH_Widget_Animation_Duration`), K1.126 (a QTabBar's tear line), K1.133† (tab move and drag-hover switch),
K1.182 (item-view drop indicator), K1.19† (KWin's quick-tile preview), K2.10 (dock title).

Application code that draws its own drop zone, so not a platform value:
- **Kate** (view spaces and tool-view sidebars): a tab dropped on another view space shows a `QRubberBand`
  over the whole view space and moves the document there (no edge zones, no split by drop):
  `kate/kateviewspace.cpp:669-675`, `:687-701`; the tool-view sidebar shows a `QRubberBand` parented to the
  main window and, for reordering, a second one forced to 4 px: `kate/katemdi.cpp:595-596`, `:1248-1282`.
  Their paint is Breeze's (K2.12, and the row below); the 4 px is Kate's.
- **Konsole** (split terminals): the half of the target terminal nearest the pointer is filled with a fixed
  `QColor(100, 100, 100, 127)`, not a palette colour: `konsole/terminalDisplay/TerminalDisplay.cpp:372-385`,
  `:801-812`. Nothing for the model to carry.
- **KDDockWidgets** (Kdenlive requires it: `find_package(KDDockWidgets-qt6 2.4.0 REQUIRED)`,
  `kdenlive/CMakeLists.txt:134`; Kdenlive keeps the default indicator type, `kdenlive/src/mainwindow.cpp:155-167`):
  the default `DropIndicatorType::Classic` (`kddw/core/ViewFactory.cpp:17`) draws the library's own PNG arrows
  (`kddw/qtwidgets/views/ClassicIndicatorsWindow.cpp:32`, `:148-149`, `:333-334`; `src/img/classic_indicators/`)
  and a `QRubberBand` (`kddw/qtwidgets/views/RubberBand.cpp:17-19`), whose paint is Breeze's (K2.12). The PNGs
  are the library's artwork, not platform data. Whether KDDockWidgets is in scope with kirigami-addons is §6.2's
  question.
- Kirigami / Qt Quick: no docking control at the pinned commits.

| ID | section | model element | Element | What is missing | Source | Nearest model field | CAPS | SRC |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

## Fonts and mirroring (2026-10-28)

A completeness check of the map found two gaps no row covered: the emoji font and
the fallback chain for scripts the UI font lacks (all six platforms), and right-to-left
layout where this file had no general row. The source abbreviations are defined in
`todo_v0.7.1_model-completeness-gap-map-seeds.md`, *Fonts and mirroring sources*.

Not covered by any row (K3.45 is antialiasing and hinting, K3.46 the QFont string's extra fields). Qt
Widgets and Qt Quick (Kirigami) share Qt's font database, so one pair of rows serves both. No Plasma
setting for the emoji family was found: whether plasma-workspace's fonts KCM writes any fontconfig rule
beyond rendering (K3.45) is to verify.

| K-FM01† | 4.3 | typography | Emoji font | For an emoji run Qt asks fontconfig for a colour font with the weak family `emoji` and requires a colour font; fontconfig resolves `emoji` through its prefer list, Noto Color Emoji first, then Apple Color Emoji, Segoe UI Emoji and others, the monochrome Noto Emoji next to last (before Android Emoji), and tags emoji with the language `und-zsye`. Which of them a system has is the distribution's choice; an application can override it (`QFontDatabase::setApplicationEmojiFontFamilies`). The model has no emoji family. | P: `qt/unix/qfontconfigdatabase.cpp:839-845`, `qt/qfontdatabase.cpp:2597-2656`, `:2969-2992`; `fc/60-generic.conf:9-47`, `fc/45-generic.conf:8-85`; R: `fc-match emoji` (the reader already runs `fc-match`, `native-theme/src/fonts.rs:283-307`; the colour property in the pattern is to verify); M: Qt probe app, the sample set, the family of each `QGlyphRun` | none (`defaults.font.family`) | C18, C25 | P, R, M (review 2026-10-28: Noto Emoji is next to last in `60-generic.conf`, not last) |

The per-language preferences are fontconfig's (`65-nonlatin.conf`) and the distribution's. The model has one family and no language. | P: `qt/unix/qfontconfigdatabase.cpp:823-885` (Han comment `:861-865`); `fc/65-nonlatin.conf` (per-language families, e.g. `:27-39`, `:102-108`); R: `fc-match -s '<family>:lang=<tag>'` per language (pattern syntax per the fontconfig user's guide, to verify); M: Qt probe app, the sample set under each `LANG` | none (`defaults.font.family`) | C18, C25 | P, R, M |

| K-P3-1† | 4.1 | [window] | Resize-only borders | Breeze adds an invisible resize band `largeSpacing` wide outside the window: above the title bar unless the window is maximised vertically; on the sides and the bottom when the border size is None (the sides only for NoSides), each dropped on a maximised axis. Over it KWin shows its resize pointer shapes (from the cursor theme, K3.64; to verify). The model has no resize zone | P: `deco:421-445` | none | C20, C22, C26 | P, V |
| K-P3-2† | 4.1 | [window] | Caption buttons per window | Breeze creates a decoration button only when the window allows its action: close (closeable), maximise (maximisable), minimise (minimisable), shade (shadeable), context help (the window provides help); each button shows or hides as that capability changes. K1.5's layout is the superset. Breeze has no separate tool-window or dialog title bar (no `tool`, `utility` or `modal` branch in `breezedecoration.cpp`, by search) | P: `dbtn:53-97` | none | C20, C13, C07 | P |
| K-P3-3† | 4.1 | [window] | Custom tiling | KWin's tiles: a window in a tile is inset by the tile **padding**, half of it between two tiles and all of it against the screen edge; the padding is kept per virtual desktop and output in kwinrc `[Tiling][<desktop id>][<output uuid>] padding`, default 4 (the older `[Tiling] padding` is the fallback); with no saved layout, three columns 25 % / 50 % / 25 %. **Shift held while dragging** a window shows the tile under the pointer with the quick-tile outline (K1.19†) and drops the window into it. **Meta+T** opens the tile editor (an effect on by default): the windows blurred (radius 64) under a `backgroundColor` layer at opacity 0.4; each tile a rectangle inset `smallSpacing`, radius `cornerRadius`, a `textColor` border with a 1 px `backgroundColor` border inside it, at opacity 0.3 (floating tiles 0.6, filled `backgroundColor`); a layout bar in the Plasma theme's `widgets/background` frame; fades and blur over `animationTime(200 ms)`, OutCubic | R: kwinrc `[Tiling]` (`kwin src/tiles/tilemanager.cpp:288-339`, default layout `:300-309`, saved `:390-404`); P: `kwin src/tiles/tile.cpp:171-182`; `kwin src/window.cpp:1095-1096`, `:1231-1241`; `kwin src/plugins/tileseditor/Main.qml:58-118`, `:176-178`; `TileDelegate.qml:107-125`; `tileseditoreffect.cpp:33`, `:53`; default-on `tileseditor/metadata.json:51` | none | C20, C26, C04, C14, C25 | R, P |
| K-P3-4† | 4.1 | [tab] | Overflow and label length | **Qt Widgets**: tab labels are never elided (`SH_TabBar_ElideMode` is `ElideNone` in QCommonStyle and Breeze does not override it), so a crowded bar scrolls with K1.125's buttons. **qqc2 TabBar**: a horizontal ListView that flicks (stopping at its ends) with no scroll buttons, snapping to tabs and keeping the current tab 40 px inside either end; tabs overlap by Breeze's `tabOverlap` | P: qtbase v6.12.0 `qcommonstyle.cpp:5253-5254`; no `SH_TabBar_ElideMode` case in `st:885-976`; `qqc2@2efe38d5 org.kde.desktop/TabBar.qml:30-48` | none | C26, C13 | P |
| K-P3-5† | 4.1 | [menu], [combo_box] | Where a popup opens against its owner | A **push button's** menu opens below the button when it fits on the screen, else above it; under right-to-left it is right-aligned with the button; in a vertical toolbar it opens beside the button. A **tool button's** menu follows the same rule. A **combo box's** list: Breeze leaves `SH_ComboBox_Popup` false (QCommonStyle's value), so the list opens **below** the field (not over the current item), else above, else on the larger side, cut to fit, showing at most `maxVisibleItems` 10 rows; the qqc2 ComboBox popup sits at the control's height (below). ## kirigami-addons (2026-10-28)

The rationale (R1.2) puts kirigami-addons in scope: KDE maintains it, and its form cards, date and time
pickers, About page and dialogs are what many KDE applications show. Surveyed at its latest release tag,
**v1.15.0** (tag object `c3416822957d683aa95607208e9db3c33170115d`, commit
`4668f4cad74205348f6111e8ef33b5192463fb80`, 2026-10-01), read 2026-10-28 from
`invent.kde.org/libraries/kirigami-addons`. **`ka/F:L`** = file `F` under the repository's `src/` folder,
line(s) `L`, at that commit. Comparisons cite `kirigami@eecf183a` and `qqc2-desktop-style@2efe38d5` as the
rows above do.

Method: all 112 QML files under `src/` and the C++ that sets a look were read (the Android Java pickers
are Android's own dialogs and out of scope). On Plasma an addons application runs the
`org.kde.desktop` style (K-P4-8†; also K-KA-47†), so every qqc2 control inside an addons component
(CheckBox, Switch, TextField, SpinBox, ComboBox, Slider, Button, ToolButton, Menu, MenuItem, ItemDelegate,
ToolTip) paints through Breeze: its look is the Breeze and Q rows above, and the rows below record only
what addons adds or changes around it. No row, because a row above covers it: `ActionMenuBar` is a qqc2
`MenuBar` (Q2.9–Q2.10) and its items qqc2 `MenuItem`s with the action's tooltip (`ActionMenuItem`);
`ActionMenu` and `ActionMenuPopup` only feed `ConvergentContextMenu` (K-KA-31†); the desktop mode of
`ConvergentContextMenu` and every `FormComboBoxDelegate` popup in ComboBox mode is a qqc2 `Menu`
(Q1.23–Q1.32); `FormPasswordFieldDelegate`'s field is Kirigami's
`PasswordField` (Q1.19); the command bar's dialog is Kirigami's `SearchDialog` (Q1.118); the About page's
"What's new" marker is Kirigami's `Badge` (Q2.25–Q2.27); `SonnetConfigPage` is built from form cards and
Kirigami's `CheckSubtitleDelegate` (Q1.87) only. Not drawn by addons: `NativeMenuBar` hands its menus to
Qt Labs Platform's native menu bar (`ka/actions/labs/NativeMenuBar.qml:32`); `DownloadAction`,
`AlbumModelItem`, `ContentItemLoader`, the settings modules (`ConfigurationModule`, `SettingAction`, …),
the models and `zxcvbn` draw nothing.
